- **issue-lifecycle**: Drive ONE Linear issue through its full lifecycle in a single session — spec (issue-spec) → human spec-approval gate → implementation (issue-implement) → PR review-feedback rounds → stop before merge. A BUG skips the spec entirely and enters at implementation, with the PR as its review surface. A THIN, event-driven orchestrator: every heavy phase runs in a fresh bounded sub-agent that returns a compact summary and exits, so the orchestrator's own token cost stays small across the whole lifecycle.
